[EVENT: IRQ44102]

Country: Iraq | Location: Baghdad
Type: Strategic developments > Change to group/activity | Disorder: Strategic developments
Actor1: Police Forces of Iraq (2022-2026) [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Sotaliraq [National]

DESCRIPTION:

The measures are intended to protect US personnel against attacks by pro-Iran groups on the anniversary of the assassination of Soleimani and Abu Muhandis. Popular Mobilization Forces units including Kataib Hezbolla, Asaib Ahl Al Haq, Badr Organization, Harakat Al Nujaba, and Kataib Imam Ali declared their intent to hold special events during the anniversary and vowed to revenge the assassination.

[EVENT: IRQ44598]
Date: 2023-01-22
Country: Iraq | Location: As Samawah
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Iraq) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Al Mirbad [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 22 January 2023, a large number of workers contracted by the Muthanna Department of Health protested in front of the Department building in As Samawah city. The protesters denounced what they referred to as manipulation of contract dates in the process intended to convert their contracts into permanent positions. The protesters stated their list of contract dates submitted by their department does not reflect their real contract dates, claiming their real contract dates qualify them for permanent positions. The protesters stated the manipulation will result in them losing out on the permanent positions because the central government has announced there will be no new appointments to permanent positions in 2023. The protesters threatened to continued protesting until the correct contracts dates are submitted.

[EVENT: IRQ44409]
Date: 2023-01-23
Country: Iraq | Location: Baghdad - Rusafa
Type: Riots > Violent demonstration | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Rioters (Iraq) [Rioters]
Actor2: Police Forces of Iraq (2022-2026) [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Al Ahad TV; Basnews; Kurdistan24; National Iraqi News Agency [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 23 January 2023, more than 400 of demonstrators gathered in front of Swedish embassy in Baghdad - Rusafa. The demonstrators denounced the burning of a Koran in front of the Turkish Embassy in Stockholm, Sweden by a far-right activist. The demonstrators demanded the boycott of Swedish goods and closure of the embassy. The demonstrators later clashed with police while trying to storm the Swedish embassy. The demonstrations began after a 24-hour ultimatum issued by Moqtada Al Sadr expired. Sadr demanded the Swedish government take action regarding the burning. Pro-Iran groups also organized the demonstration. 7 demonstrators and 1 policeperson was injured.

[EVENT: IRQ44686]

Actor2: Civilians (Iraq) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Kurdistan24; Rudaw; Sotaliraq [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 1 February 2023, Al Asadi tribal militants abducted a civilian from an unspecified tribe who is the head of Nature Iraq on the road between Babil and Baghdad province 5km north of Baghdad city. Nature Iraq is an accredited program of the UNEP. The victim was travelling with his cousin when two cars with militants in civilian clothes stopped them and abducted the head of Nature Iraq. They tied up the cousin and left him in the car. The victim's family believes they know which group was behind the abduction but refused to disclose this adding they have not received a ransom demand. Local authorities ruled out the possibility that the abduction was motivated over a dispute relating to the Damaj Marshland.

Actor1: Military Forces of Turkey (2016-) [External/Other forces]
Actor2: PKK: Kurdistan Workers Party [Rebel group]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: ANF News; People's Defense Forces [Other-International]

DESCRIPTION:
On 21 February 2023, PKK militants attacked Turkish soldiers, who were attempting to place explosives in PKK positions, via firearms in the Cemco (Cheme Ci) area in Al Amadiya district, Duhok, in conjunction with shelling carried out by Turkish military. HPG (PKK) claimed that Turkish forces used bombs containing poisonous gas during the shelling, but this was not confirmed by other sources. Casualties unknown.
